# Graph Report - thingtime-chatgpt-plan  (2026-10-18)

## Corpus Check
- 3882 files · ~9,965,990 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 84 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 28, .css 16, .entitlements 8)

## Summary
- 57161 nodes · 161181 edges · 1192 communities (1101 shown, 91 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 5659 edges (avg confidence: 0.88)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `f9281273`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
